# Archive {.unnumbered}

The site always shows the current workbook. An **edition** is a fixed copy of it: a tag on the repository, named for the month it was cut (`edition-2026.10`; a second cut in the same month is `edition-2026.10.1`), and a GitHub Release that keeps that edition's built files: the Word editions of the books, the presentation decks, the narration audio and the videos. Nothing in a Release changes after it is published, so a link to an edition's file, or to the source at an edition, keeps pointing at the same thing.

Each book, guide and developer workbook ends with its **version and revision history**: version 1.0 in the first edition it appears in, one step up in each edition that changes it, and the changes in each. Every change between editions, however small, is in the repository's [commit history](https://github.com/WhalerMike/modernization-workbook/commits/main).

| [2026.10](#edition-2026-10) | 2026-10-31 | The first edition: the 19 books, six companion guides and three developer workbooks as of October 31, 2026, with every presentation deck, narrated deck and narration audio file. | [430 files](https://github.com/WhalerMike/modernization-workbook/releases/tag/edition-2026.10) |

### Edition 2026.11.5 (Release 2.0, the baseline) {#edition-2026-11-5}

November 8, 2026. Release 2.0 is the workbook's baseline edition: the nineteen topic books, and the guides, developer workbooks, training pages and Source Register approved by the workbook's editor, are complete and checked against every other book. From here every change is tracked against Release 2.0 (Version 2.1, 2.2 and on); Baseline is not a claim of independent peer review.

### Edition 2026.11.3 {#edition-2026-11-3}

November 5, 2026. Adds the workbook's Windows Server planning standard (Windows Server 2016 gone by January 12, 2027, Server 2019 as the minimum), slide source links through the Source Register in ten decks, narration that names the original source, and the training module decks.

### Edition 2026.11.2 {#edition-2026-11-2}

November 2, 2026. Adds the deployment runbooks for all five kits, federal requirements and KSIs for every scripted step, the Privileged Access, Attribute-Driven Migration and Non-Person Identity learning paths, the Privileged Access implementation guide, and the HR tours, the Ticket-to-Machine and HR module decks and the Non-Person Identity guide decks.

- **Release:** [edition-2026.11.2](https://github.com/WhalerMike/modernization-workbook/releases/tag/edition-2026.11.2) · [source at this edition](https://github.com/WhalerMike/modernization-workbook/tree/edition-2026.11.2)

### Edition 2026.11.1 {#edition-2026-11-1}

November 1, 2026. Adds the Ticket-to-Machine story deck and eight chapter tours, the completed Microsoft Automation learning path with a narrated deck per module, and a two-part narrated deck for the Cloud DNS evaluation.

### Edition 2026.11 {#edition-2026-11}

November 1, 2026. Adds the HR-Driven Identity and Ticket-to-Machine implementation guides with their developer workbooks, learning paths and gate-level narrated deck series; retires Research into guides, training, reference, evaluations and explainers; restores the developer workbook as Book 19; and adds the content rules and license.
